   99  void FrameTargeter::beginFrame(const BeginFrameArgs& args, const IVsyncSource& vsyncSource,
  100                                 IsFencePendingFuncPtr isFencePendingFuncPtr) {
  101      mVsyncId = args.vsyncId;
  102      mFrameBeginTime = args.frameBeginTime;
  103      mDebugPresentTimeDelay = args.debugPresentTimeDelay;
  104  
  105      // The `expectedVsyncTime`, which was predicted when this frame was scheduled, is normally in
  106      // the future relative to `frameBeginTime`, but may not be for delayed frames. Adjust
  107      // `mExpectedPresentTime` accordingly, but not `mScheduledPresentTime`.
  108      const TimePoint lastScheduledPresentTime = mScheduledPresentTime;
  109      mScheduledPresentTime = args.expectedVsyncTime;
  110  
  111      const Period vsyncPeriod = vsyncSource.period();
  112      const Period minFramePeriod = vsyncSource.minFramePeriod();
  113  
  114      // Calculate the expected present time once and use the cached value throughout this frame to
  115      // make sure all layers are seeing this same value.
  116      if (args.expectedVsyncTime >= args.frameBeginTime) {
  117          mExpectedPresentTime = args.expectedVsyncTime;
  118      } else {
  119          mExpectedPresentTime = vsyncSource.vsyncDeadlineAfter(args.frameBeginTime);
  120          if (args.sfWorkDuration > vsyncPeriod) {
  121              // Inflate the expected present time if we're targeting the next VSYNC.
  122              mExpectedPresentTime += vsyncPeriod;
  123          }
  124      }

E328　native/services/surfaceflinger/FrontEnd/TransactionHandler.cpp:67–85
   67      // Transactions with a buffer pending on a barrier may be on a different applyToken
   68      // than the transaction which satisfies our barrier. In fact this is the exact use case
   69      // that the primitive is designed for. This means we may first process
   70      // the barrier dependent transaction, determine it ineligible to complete
   71      // and then satisfy in a later inner iteration of flushPendingTransactionQueues.
   72      // The barrier dependent transaction was eligible to be presented in this frame
   73      // but we would have prevented it without case. To fix this we continually
   74      // loop through flushPendingTransactionQueues until we perform an iteration
   75      // where the number of transactionsPendingBarrier doesn't change. This way
   76      // we can continue to resolve dependency chains of barriers as far as possible.
   77      int lastTransactionsPendingBarrier = 0;
   78      int transactionsPendingBarrier = 0;
   79      do {
   80          lastTransactionsPendingBarrier = transactionsPendingBarrier;
   81          // Collect transactions that are ready to be applied.
   82          transactionsPendingBarrier = flushPendingTransactionQueues(transactions, flushState);
   83      } while (lastTransactionsPendingBarrier != transactionsPendingBarrier);
   84  
   85      applyUnsignaledBufferTransaction(transactions, flushState);

34954      protected void votePreferredFrameRate() {
34955          // use toolkitSetFrameRate flag to gate the change

34964                  && (frameRate > 0 || (mAttachInfo.mViewVelocityApi && velocity > 0f))) {
34965              float velocityFrameRate = 0f;
34966              if (mAttachInfo.mViewVelocityApi && velocity > 0f) {
34967                  velocityFrameRate = convertVelocityToFrameRate(velocity);
34968              }
34969              int compatibility;
34970              float frameRateToSet;
34971              if (frameRate >= velocityFrameRate) {
34972                  compatibility = FRAME_RATE_COMPATIBILITY_FIXED_SOURCE;
34973                  frameRateToSet = frameRate;
34974              } else {
34975                  compatibility = FRAME_RATE_COMPATIBILITY_AT_LEAST;
34976                  frameRateToSet = velocityFrameRate;
34977              }
34978              viewRootImpl.votePreferredFrameRate(frameRateToSet, compatibility);
